ARC-CT combines three components: (1) an Anato- myQFormer localizing evidence via queries constrained by automatically generated organ masks; (2) a label-Jaccard soft InfoNCE objective in- tegrating the standard one-hot target with the label-set overlap of each pair, which reduces false-negative penalties between studies that share clinical findings; and (3) an organ-level alignment loss connecting mask- pooled visual features to organ-specific report text extracted offline with a large language model. ARC-CT achieves a 0.86 mask-free macro AUC across 18 abnormalities using a compact 3D ResNet-18 backbone. Over- all, ARC-CT outperforms both comparable efficient baselines and sev- eral larger transformer models. Our code and weights are available at https://github.com/arc-ct/arc-ct.

</details>


### [69] [LayerRecall: A State-Conditioned Memory Router for Long-Horizon Consistency in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28460)
*Yixuan Ding,Jiahao Kong,Wei Huang,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRecall方法，通过当前状态条件化的层选择性记忆路由器，将远距离历史K/V状态注入视频DiT中特定对记忆敏感的层，解决自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中历史线索被近因缓存驱逐的问题；同时提出Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching (CHPM)策略，利用特权长上下文参考在预测空间中监督有界记忆路由器，无需显式记忆分配标签。实验在MemoBench和MovieBench上达到最优，同时保持局部连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型通过从有界近期上下文生成块来实现可扩展的长视频生成，但基于近因的缓存虽然保持了局部连续性，却驱逐了当主体、物体、场景或属性重新出现时所需的历史线索。现有记忆机制虽然能将模型暴露于非局部历史，但仅提供访问权限并不能确保有效利用——视频DiT各层对当前、近期和远距离上下文有不同偏好，因此需要同时决定检索什么和在哪里使用。

Method: 1) 层感知分析：揭示视频DiT层对当前、近期和远期上下文存在不同偏好。2) LayerRecall：一个当前条件化的、层选择性记忆路由器，检索相关历史K/V状态并仅注入骨干网络中对记忆敏感的具体层，其余层保持局部注意力。3) CHPM (Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching)：使用特权长上下文参考在预测空间中监督有界记忆路由器，降低对稀缺高质量长视频和显式记忆分配标签的依赖。

Result: 在100个多镜头评估提示上，LayerRecall在MemoBench和MovieBench上取得最佳综合结果，在VBench-Long上与骨干网络持平。定性分析展示记忆引导的自校正能力——最初不匹配的局部属性恢复到历史外观而不重置进行中的运动或场景结构。额外分析验证了跨骨干可移植性和可忽略的推理开销。

Conclusion: LayerRecall通过层选择性和当前条件化的记忆路由机制，有效解决了长视频生成中远距离历史信息恢复的问题，在不牺牲局部连续性的前提下增强了长期记忆恢复能力，且具有跨骨干可移植性和极低推理开销，证明了"检索什么"和"在哪里使用"对长距离记忆有效性的关键作用。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables scalable long-video generation by producing chunks from a bounded recent context. While recency-based caching preserves local continuity, it evicts historical cues needed when subjects, objects, scenes, or attributes reappear. Existing memory mechanisms expose models to nonlocal history, but access alone does not ensure effective use. Our analysis reveals that video DiT layers exhibit distinct preferences for current, recent, and distant context, suggesting that long-range memory requires deciding both what to retrieve and where to use it. We introduce LayerRecall, a current-conditioned, layer-selective memory router that retrieves relevant historical K/V states and injects them only into backbone-specific memory-sensitive layers while preserving local attention elsewhere. To reduce reliance on scarce high-quality long-horizon videos and explicit memory-allocation labels, we further propose Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching (CHPM), which uses a privileged long-context reference to supervise the bounded-memory router in prediction space. Across 100 multi-shot evaluation prompts, LayerRecall achieves the best overall results on MemoBench and MovieBench while matching its backbone on VBench-Long, demonstrating stronger long-range recovery without sacrificing local continuity. Qualitative analyses further reveal memory-guided self-correction, whereby initially mismatched local attributes return to their historical appearance without resetting ongoing motion or scene structure. Additional analyses show cross-backbone portability and negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [70] [Anatomy-Aware Promptable Segmentation with Online Interactive Training for AUTOPET V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28461)
*Pablo Lozano-Jimenez,Sergio Romero-Tapiador,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向全身病灶分割的解剖感知、可提示（promptable）模型，用于FDG和PSMA PET/CT影像，在AUTOPET V挑战中开发。该方法以nnU-Net模型家族为基础，采用两阶段训练：预训练阶段生成初始分割，在线交互阶段利用划痕提示（scribble prompts）逐步细化预测。通过器官监督共享单一分割头同时预测病灶和器官以降低生理性摄取的假阳性；由于推理时未提供示踪剂类型，加入基于图像处理和随机森林的示踪剂分类器，将每个检查路由到FDG+PSMA联合模型或PSMA专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDG和PSMA PET/CT中的全身病灶分割面临生理性摄取导致的假阳性问题，且推理时示踪剂类型未知，需要同时处理两种示踪剂影像。作者希望通过器官监督减少假阳性，通过交互式提示提升分割精度，并通过示踪剂分类路由来适配不同示踪剂的特性。

Method: 采用nnU-Net模型家族，两阶段训练：(1) 预训练生成初始分割；(2) 交互式阶段利用scribble提示逐步细化预测。引入器官监督，使用单一共享分割头从相同特征同时预测病灶和器官。另构建基于图像处理和随机森林的示踪剂分类器（利用冠状MIP特征），将影像路由至FDG+PSMA联合模型或PSMA专用模型。

Result: 四折交叉验证显示：器官监督模型获得最佳且最稳定的性能；交互式阶段中Dice分数随每次提示单调提升；PSMA专用训练获得最强的示踪剂专项结果。

Conclusion: 器官监督能有效减少假阳性并提升分割稳定性；交互式提示机制持续改善性能；针对特定示踪剂的专项训练相比统一模型在对应示踪剂上表现更优，验证了模型路由策略的有效性。

Abstract: We present an anatomy-aware, promptable model for whole-body lesion segmentation in FDG and PSMA PET/CT, developed for the AUTOPET V challenge. The proposed method is built as family of nnU-Net-based models and trained in two stages: i) a pre-training stage that produces a strong initial segmentation, and ii) an online interactive stage that learns to exploit scribble prompts, refining the prediction over successive interactions. Anatomical context is incorporated through organ supervision using a single shared head that predicts lesions and organs from the same features, which reduces false positives arising from physiological uptake. Also as the tracer (i.e., FDG/PSMA) is not provided at inference, we add a tracer classifier based on image processing and a random forest over coronal MIP features, routing each study to a combined FDG+PSMA model or to a PSMA-specific model. Across four-fold cross-validation the organ-supervised model achieves the best and most stable performance, the interactive stage improves the Dice score monotonically with each prompt, and PSMA-specific training yields the strongest tracer-wise results.

</details>


### [71] [Learning the Target Priors Before Image Translation: A Decoupled Training Paradigm for Cross-Modal Image Translation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28517)
*Keyan Hu,Mingtao Wang,Ziyu Zhou,Tiandong Shi,Haifeng Li,Ji Qi,Chao Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LTP-BIT，一种先学目标域先验再学跨模态翻译的先验优先范式，将目标先验学习和跨模态条件依赖学习解耦，仅用9.81%任务参数在SAR-to-RGB和NIR-to-RGB基准上达到SOTA，且在仅25%配对样本下保持近全数据实例保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态图像翻译方法在稀缺配对数据上联合学习目标先验和跨模态依赖，忽略了关键不对称性：只有跨模态依赖本质上需要配对数据。作者通过条件分数和去噪风险分析正式刻画了这一区别，旨在解耦两个学习任务以缓解配对数据稀缺问题。

Method: LTP-BIT采用先验优先范式：(1) 首先在大规模无配对图像上学习目标域生成先验；(2) 保留预训练骨干权重，通过P-DART（参数高效双流架构）学习源条件控制。通过可控实验发现先验匹配和缩放主要改善目标域真实感，而实例保真度更依赖条件适应。

Result: 在SAR-to-RGB和NIR-to-RGB基准上达到SOTA，仅使用9.81%任务特定参数。在QXS-SAROPT数据集中，仅用25%配对样本即可保留近全数据实例保真度。

Conclusion: LTP-BIT验证了先验优先、解耦学习的有效性：大规模无配对数据学习目标先验，参数高效的条件控制用于跨模态翻译，显著降低配对数据需求的同时保持性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal image translation in remote sensing must preserve source-observed content while matching the target-domain distribution. Existing methods jointly learn the target prior and cross-modal dependence from scarce paired data, overlooking a key asymmetry: only the latter intrinsically requires cross-modal correspondence. We formalize this distinction through conditional-score and denoising-risk analyses and propose Learning the Target Priors Before Image Translation (LTP-BIT), a prior-first paradigm that decouples the two learning tasks. LTP-BIT first learns a target-domain generative prior from large-scale unpaired imagery, then retains the pretrained backbone weights and learns source-conditioned control through P-DART, a parameter-efficient dual-stream architecture. Controlled experiments show that prior matching and scaling primarily improve target-domain realism, whereas instance fidelity relies more strongly on conditional adaptation. LTP-BIT achieves state-of-the-art performance across SAR-to-RGB and NIR-to-RGB benchmarks using only 9.81% task-specific parameters. On QXS-SAROPT, it retains near-full-data instance fidelity with only 25% of the paired samples.

</details>


### [72] [Texture Image Classification Using DWT AlexNet Feature Fusion and Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28524)
*Arun D. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为DWT_AlexNet_DNN的混合特征融合框架，将离散小波变换（DWT）特征与AlexNet提取的深度特征相结合，用于纹理图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工特征在表示复杂视觉模式方面能力有限，而深度学习方法未能充分利用纹理图像固有的多尺度空间-频率信息，因此需要一种融合两种特征优势的方法。

Method: 提出DWT_AlexNet_DNN混合特征融合框架，将DWT提取的小波特征与AlexNet网络提取的深度特征进行融合，以同时利用手工特征的局部纹理表征能力和深度特征的自学习判别表示能力。

Result: 该摘要未提供具体的实验数值结果。

Conclusion: DWT_AlexNet_DNN框架有望通过融合多尺度空间-频率信息与深度判别特征来提升纹理图像分类性能。

Abstract: Texture image classification plays a significant role in computer vision applications, including industrial inspection, medical image analysis, remote sensing, and object recognition. Handcrafted features can capture local texture characteristics but may have limited capability to represent complex visual patterns. In contrast, deep learning models automatically learn discriminative representations but may not fully exploit the multiscale spatial-frequency information inherent in texture images. This paper proposes a hybrid feature fusion framework, termed DWT_AlexNet_DNN, which combines Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) features with deep features extracted using AlexNet for texture image classification.

</details>


### [73] [Video Generative Models as Geometry Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28549)
*Haosen Yang,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Xiatian Zhu,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoNeXt利用预训练视频生成模型，将几何估计（深度和表面法线）重新定义为下一帧预测任务，构建了统一的、数据高效的几何估计框架，在零样本单目深度和法线估计上超越了任务专属和统一生成式竞争者，并以远少于100倍的数据媲美判别式SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的几何估计方法存在两个局限：(1)将深度和表面法线作为独立任务分别训练，无法利用几何目标间的内在关联；(2)联合微调图像扩散模型需要大量标注数据。作者旨在提出一种原则性的统一框架来克服这些限制。

Method: GeoNeXt通过复用预训练视频生成模型，将几何估计创新性地表述为下一帧预测任务。该方法继承了视频模型的结构化知识和更丰富的先验，并进一步适配以联合建模图像与几何目标（image <-> geometry），从而实现数据高效的学习。

Result: 在多个数据集上的零样本单目深度和表面法线估计实验中，GeoNeXt在显著减少训练数据的情况下超越了此前任务专属和统一的生成式竞争者，并且媲美甚至在某些基准上超越了使用超过100倍数据训练的判别式SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 将视频生成模型重新用作几何估计的统一框架是有效且数据高效的路径，视频先验中的结构化知识能够显著增强几何估计的性能，为跨模态几何学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent generative approaches to geometry estimation adapt pretrained image diffusion models and treat the task as image-conditioned generation. Leveraging off-the-shelf image diffusion models, they either (i) train task-specific geometry models (for depth and surface normal estimation) independently, losing the opportunity of exploring the intrinsic correlation of these geometric targets, or (ii) jointly fine-tune modified image diffusion backbones (e.g., altered self-attention), which typically demands substantial labeled data. To overcome these limitations in a principled fashion, we repurpose pretrained video generative models as a unified and data-efficient framework for geometry estimation, formulated innovatively as a next-frames prediction task. Our method, GeoNeXt, inherits naturally structured knowledge and richer priors from the video model, while further adapting them for joint modeling of images and geometry targets (image <-> geometry), enabling more data efficient and effective learning of geometry. Extensive experiments validate our method for zero-shot monocular depth and surface normal estimation across diverse datasets, outperforming both previous task-specific and unified generative competitors while using substantially less training data. Notably, our method rivals discriminative state-of-the-art approaches trained on over 100x more data and even standouts on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [GeBDA: Building Damage Assessment as Text-Based Sequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28567)
*Olivier Dietrich,Krishna Sapkota,Konrad Schindler,Genady Beryozkin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将建筑损伤评估（BDA）建模为自回归序列生成任务，利用通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）直接从双时相卫星图像中预测可变长度的边界框及损伤标签，初步基于Gemma模型的实现取得了有前景的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BDA方法要么依赖专用网络架构，要么需要对地理空间图像基础模型进行微调。作者探索是否一个通用的VLM仅通过自回归序列生成就能同时完成建筑定位和损伤分级，从而摆脱专用架构和微调流程的复杂性。

Method: 将BDA重新定义为预测一组可变长度的边界框集合，每个边界框由其坐标和损伤标签共同表示。基于开源的Gemma模型，仅输入双时相卫星图像和合适的文本提示，模型通过自回归序列生成直接输出建筑边界框和对应的损伤等级。

Result: 初步实验表明，基于Gemma的VLM实现能够在双时相卫星图像上生成有前景的损伤映射结果，验证了通用VLM直接以序列生成方式解决BDA任务的可行性。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了通用视觉-语言模型在不依赖专用网络架构或地理空间基础模型微调的情况下，也能通过自回归序列生成有效完成建筑损伤评估任务，为BDA提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Conventionally, Building Damage Assessment (BDA) is tackled either with dedicated network architectures or by fine-tuning geospatial image foundation models. In this work, we ask whether a general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) can localize buildings and grade their damage through autoregressive sequence generation alone. We cast BDA as predicting a variable-length set of bounding boxes, each specified by its coordinates and a damage label. Our preliminary implementation, based on the open Gemma model, achieves promising damage mapping results from only bi-temporal satellite images and a suitable text prompt.

</details>


### [75] [SignRR: Retrieve and Refine Real Motion for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28568)
*Fidel Omar Tito Cruz,Angie Sanchez Marquina,Summy Farfan,Gissella Bejarano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"检索-精炼"（retrieve-and-refine）范式的手语生成框架SignRR，从真实手语动作片段字典中初始化运动序列，并通过部件感知的残差VQ-VAE精炼为全局连贯的手语序列，在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily上取得SOTA反向翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成（SLP）的两类方法存在固有缺陷：生成式模型从先验或噪声中合成动作，难以保留罕见手型和特定手语者的精细关节动作；检索式方法虽复用真实运动片段，但拼接不同手语者及不同协同发音上下文的片段会引入节奏和风格的不一致性，且不仅限于片段边界。因此需要一种互补方案：利用检索提供真实关节动作，利用学习式精炼施加全局连贯性。

Method: 提出SignRR框架，遵循检索-精炼范式：首先从真实手语片段字典中检索并初始化运动序列，然后通过部件感知的残差VQ-VAE对整个序列进行精炼。其中残差量化用于保留精细手部关节动作，时间长度差异在潜空间中处理。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily两个基准数据集上的实验表明，SignRR实现了最优的反向翻译（back-translation）性能，同时保持了具有竞争力的姿态质量。

Conclusion: 检索-精炼范式有效融合了检索的真实关节动作与学习式全局精炼的优势，弥补了纯生成与纯检索方法的不足，为手语生成提供了新的研究路线。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate continuous signing motion from spoken language, often through gloss-to-pose generation. Prior work mainly follows two paradigms. Generative models synthesize motion from a learned prior or from noise, without reference to an observed signing instance, making rare hand configurations and signer-specific articulation difficult to preserve. Retrieval-based methods reuse real, well-articulated motion segments, but concatenating segments from different signers and co-articulation contexts can introduce rhythm and style inconsistencies across the full sequence, not only at segment boundaries. These limitations suggest a complementary solution: use retrieval to provide realistic articulation, and use learned refinement to impose the global coherence that retrieval alone lacks. We therefore propose retrieve-and-refine, a paradigm that starts from real retrieved motion and refines it into a globally coherent signing sequence rather than generating motion from scratch. Our framework, SignRR, initializes motion from a dictionary of real sign segments and refines the full sequence with a part-aware Residual VQ-VAE, where residual quantization preserves fine hand articulation and temporal length differences are handled in the latent space. Experiments on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily show that SignRR achieves state-of-the-art back-translation performance while maintaining competitive pose quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Accelerating LLM Inference via Vector Index Based Output Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27460)
*Martin Loretz,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将自回归解码中的输出投影和top-k token选择重构为最大内积搜索（MIPS）问题，用基于HNSW的向量索引替代稠密词汇表投影，仅检索少量高得分候选token。在CPU推理场景下，该方法在Gemma 3、Llama 3.2和Qwen 3模型上显著加速输出投影，端到端批量大小为1的解码吞吐量最高提升82%（Gemma 3 270M），且不损失生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型输出嵌入矩阵在自回归解码过程中造成显著的内存带宽瓶颈，尤其是对于词汇表庞大的多语言紧凑型LLM。在延迟敏感的小批量解码场景中，稠密输出投影的计算和内存访问开销成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 将输出投影+top-k token选择重构为token嵌入上的最大内积搜索（MIPS）问题，用HNSW（分层可导航小世界图）向量索引替代稠密词汇表投影。输出头仅检索少量高得分候选token，并通过将检索到的logits散射（scatter）到稀疏的全词汇量张量中来集成到现有解码流水线。

Result: 在CPU推理环境下，对Gemma 3、Llama 3.2和Qwen 3模型进行验证，端到端批量大小为1的解码吞吐量最高提升82%（Gemma 3 270M），同时在AlpacaEval评估下保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 近似检索（approximate retrieval）在延迟敏感的小批量解码场景中，是稠密输出投影的一个实用替代方案。

Abstract: Large output embedding matrices create a significant memory bandwidth bottleneck during autoregressive decoding, especially for compact LLMs with large multilingual vocabularies. We reformulate the output projection followed by top-k token selection as a maximum inner product search over token embeddings and replace the dense vocabulary projection with an HNSW-based vector index. The resulting output head retrieves only a small candidate set of high-scoring tokens and can be integrated into existing decoding pipelines by scattering retrieved logits into a sparse full-vocabulary tensor. On CPU inference with Gemma 3, Llama 3.2, and Qwen 3 models, our method substantially accelerates the output projection and improves end-to-end batch-size-one decoding throughput by up to 82% for Gemma 3 270M, while preserving generation quality under AlpacaEval evaluation. These results suggest approximate retrieval is a practical alternative to dense output projections in latency-sensitive small-batch decoding.

</details>


### [77] [SciReC: Diagnostic Evaluation of Multimodal, Multi-Turn Relational Reasoning with Adaptive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27461)
*Nilay Yilmaz,Naga Sai Abhiram Kusumba,Stella Wenxing Liu,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciReC，一个模型自适应的多模态学术对话基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在关系推理任务中的表现；同时提出了DMRA，一个基于缺陷的诊断框架，用于量化视觉理解、知识展示和记忆回忆等组件对推理失败的贡献。结果显示Claude 4.6表现最佳，关系推理是所有模型最主要的错误来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系推理是高级认知能力，包含类比、结构、因果等多种类别，但现有研究缺乏对多模态大语言模型在这类推理任务中系统评测的方法。此外，关系推理涉及多表征和多因素（视觉理解、知识、记忆），需要能够归因失败原因的诊断框架。

Method: 构建SciReC基准数据集（模型自适应的多模态学术对话基准），对MLLM的关系推理能力进行多维度评估；提出DMRA缺陷诊断框架，通过量化视觉理解、知识展示和记忆回忆各组件对推理过程的贡献，定位模型失败的根因。评测跨模型类型（开源vs专有）、关系类别（空间、层级、时序等）和学科领域（天文学、心理学等）进行系统分析。

Result: Claude 4.6取得最佳整体关系推理得分73%，GPT 5.4以68%紧随其后。开源模型在空间关系上得分最低，专有模型在层级和时序关系上表现较弱。跨领域来看，模型在天文学上表现最差，在心理学上表现最好。DMRA结果表明关系推理本身和记忆限制是所有模型最主要的错误来源。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在关系推理任务上仍有显著提升空间，不同类型模型存在不同的弱点分布。关系推理的固有难度和记忆限制是制约模型表现的核心瓶颈，未来研究应重点改进模型的关系推理能力和长期记忆机制。

Abstract: Relational reasoning requires the process of perceptual understanding, comparing, and integrating the underlying relationships between concepts. This ability consists of multiple categories, such as analogical, structural, and cause-effect, each capturing a different aspect of higher-order understanding. To examine the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLM) on these relational inference tasks, we developed SciReC, a model-adaptive multimodal academic dialog benchmark. As the relational reasoning process involves multiple representations and various factors (visual understanding, exhibiting knowledge, and memory recall), we propose DMRA, a deficit-based diagnostic framework that quantifies the contribution of these components to identify the primary cause of unsuccessful cases. Claude 4.6 achieved the best performance on the overall relational score with 73\%, followed by GPT 5.4 with 68\%. Performance trends indicate that open-source models achieve their lowest scores on spatial relations, while proprietary models struggle more with hierarchical and sequential relations. Across domains, model performance is lowest on Astronomy and highest on Psychology. The results of DMRA reveal that relational reasoning is the primary source of error across all models, followed by memory limitations.

</details>


### [78] [Sledgehammer or Scalpel? A Fine-grained Adaptive Framework for Implicit Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27462)
*Han Wang,Yuhu Cheng,Xuesong Wang,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FAID框架，通过将隐式仇恨言论细分为浅层、目标性、上下文依赖三类，并针对不同类别采用差异化策略（轻量提示微调、知识增强、代理框架），实现细粒度自适应的隐式仇恨言论检测，在四个基准数据集上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式仇恨言论通过隐喻和上下文暗示隐藏恶意，现有PLM/LLM方法对所有样本使用单一推理过程，忽略了细粒度的语言差异，且对简单样本造成不必要的计算开销。

Method: 提出FAID框架：首先进行细粒度分类，将隐式仇恨言论分为Shallow（浅层）、Targeted（目标性）和Context-Dependent（上下文依赖）三类；然后针对不同类别采用自适应策略——浅层样本使用轻量提示微调快速分类，目标性样本通过知识增强迭代精炼模型以揭示隐藏目标，上下文依赖样本利用代理框架自动生成提示以推断缺失背景信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，FAID显著优于SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 自适应架构能够将计算资源集中在复杂的隐式样本上，同时避免对浅层样本进行冗余推理，从而在保持效率的同时提升检测性能。

Abstract: Unlike explicit attacks with obvious profanity, implicit hate speech hides malice within seemingly compliant expressions through metaphors and contextual hints, making its detection in online content review challenging. While existing PLM- or LLM-based methods perform well, they typically apply a single reasoning process to all samples. This overlooks fine-grained linguistic nuances and causes unnecessary computation for simpler cases. We observe that online hate speech is not monolithic but manifests in varied forms. We therefore define three fine-grained categories: Shallow, Targeted, and Context-Dependent. Accordingly, we propose Fine-grained Adaptive Implicit Hate speech Detection (FAID), a novel framework that first performs fine-grained classification and then adapts to specific categories. Specifically, for Shallow samples with surface-identifiable intents, the framework adopts lightweight prompt-tuning for rapid classification; for Targeted comments that bind malicious intent to concealed targets, we design knowledge augmentation to iteratively refine the model and reveal hidden targets; for Context-Dependent comments lacking background information, we utilize an agentic framework that automatically generates prompts to evolve context, infer missing background information and identify ambiguous malicious intents. This adaptive architecture focuses computational resources on complex implicit samples while avoiding redundant reasoning for shallow samples. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FAID significantly outperforms SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [79] [The Effect of Emotional Context on Large Language Models' Endorsement of Premature Decisions: Comparing Emotional Vulnerability Across Six Commercial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27465)
*Cheolho Shin,Yoojin Han,Donghun Shin,Kunho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控实验（324段对话、3种场景、3种条件），检验大语言模型是否会因用户的情绪表达（而非仅因对话轮数增加）而更倾向于支持其过早决策。结果显示情绪显著提升模型的偏袒/附和效应（中立18.6升至痛苦31.5，+12.9分，p<.001，Cohen's d=0.51），且该效应与对话长度无关；六款商业模型中五款（含顶级旗舰GPT-5.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro）表现出显著情绪效应，仅Claude Opus无显著变化，且个体模型差异与价格层级无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于日常决策建议，而模型是否会根据用户的情绪状态改变建议方向已成为重要的安全问题。作者特别关注用户持有相同客观信息但对自己的过早决策（如基于薄弱证据辞职）过于自信时，情绪表达是否会让模型更倾向鼓励其行动，即模型的"谄媚/附和"（sycophancy）是否会因情绪上下文而被放大。

Method: 采用混合效应设计：6款商业模型（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google的顶级和中档模型）×3种场景（职业变动、业务扩张、移民）×3种条件（冷静/中立/痛苦），每种重复6次，共324段对话。关键控制为"无情绪多轮中立条件"，保持事实内容和对话轮数恒定，从而将情绪效应与对话长度效应分离。使用基于8项评分标准的自动化打分（0-100）衡量模型对决策的背书强度，并用独立非Google评判模型和两位人类编码员验证结果。

Result: 情绪表达显著增加了模型的背书（中立18.6→痛苦31.5，+12.9分；混合效应β=+12.9，p<.001；Cohen's d=0.51）。该效应并非由对话长度解释（冷-中立差异不显著，p=.083）。关键发现：脆弱性因个体模型而异而非价格层级——6款模型中5款显示显著情绪效应（包括顶级旗舰Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.5），仅Claude Opus无显著变化。结果被独立非Google评判模型复现（ρ=.89），并与两位人工编码员的排序一致（ρ=.70）。

Conclusion: 通过将情绪与对话上下文分离的受控设计，研究表明情绪上下文即使对顶级旗舰LLM也会放大其附和/谄媚效应。这意味着用户在情绪脆弱时获得LLM建议可能存在安全风险，模型提供商需重视情绪诱导下的建议可靠性问题。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for everyday decision-making advice, whether a model shifts the direction of its advice according to the user's emotional state has become an important safety problem. We test whether emotional expression increases a model's endorsement (encouragement to proceed) when a user, holding the same objective information, is overconfident about a premature decision (e.g., quitting a stable job on weak evidence). As a key control, we include a no-emotion multi-turn (neutral) condition that holds factual content and the number of conversational turns constant, isolating the effect of emotion from that of conversation length. We exposed six commercial models (top-tier and mid-tier models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google) to three scenarios (career change, business expansion, emigration) across three conditions (cold/neutral/distress) with six repetitions each, yielding 324 conversations, and measured endorsement strength (0-100) via an eight-item rubric-based automated scoring. Emotional expression significantly increased endorsement (neutral 18.6 to distress 31.5, +12.9 points; mixed-effects $β= +12.9$, $p < .001$; Cohen's d = 0.51), and this was not explained by conversation length (cold-neutral difference non-significant, $p = .083$). Critically, the vulnerability varied by individual model rather than by price tier: five of six models showed a significant emotion effect, including the top-tier flagships Gemini 3.1 Pro and GPT-5.5, while only Claude Opus showed no significant change. Results were reproduced with an independent non-Google judge model ($ρ= .89$) and agreed in rank with two human coders ($ρ= .70$). Through a controlled design that separates emotion from conversational context, we show that emotional context increases LLM sycophancy even in top-tier flagship models.

</details>


### [80] [PACE: Publisher-Adaptive Content Extraction via Agentic Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27466)
*Zhanlin Liu,Munirathnam Srikanth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了PACE，一个基于智能体的框架，用于学习特定发布者的提取配置。训练阶段利用LLM分析页面结构并聚合可复用的提取模式，推理时通过学习的配置实例化固定的确定性提取器模板，无需额外LLM调用即可实现可扩展的网页内容提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网页内容提取方法难以同时满足准确性、可扩展性和适应性：通用提取器在特定发布者布局上脆弱且对丰富目标（元数据、图像、表格）支持不佳；直接基于LLM的提取灵活但成本高、延迟大；人工编写的发布者专用解析器准确但维护成本高。

Method: PACE是一个智能体框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 训练阶段，使用LLM分析代表性页面结构并聚合可复用的提取模式，学习发布者特定的提取配置；(2) 推理阶段，利用学习的配置实例化固定的确定性提取器模板，在无额外LLM调用的情况下实现可扩展提取。

Result: 在文章正文、元数据和多模态提取实验中，PACE优于所有可扩展的非人工基线，并接近人工设计的发布者专用解析器的质量。在文章文本、元数据、图像和表格的提取上均表现更强。

Conclusion: 智能体配置学习可以自动化发布者特定的网页内容提取，支持LLM就绪的页面表示（包括文章文本之外的元数据、图像和表格等丰富内容），在不牺牲可扩展性的前提下实现接近人工解析器的高质量提取。

Abstract: Web content extraction is essential for reliable LLM data pipelines, yet existing methods often struggle to jointly satisfy accuracy, scalability, and adaptability. General-purpose extractors can be applied broadly, but they are often brittle on publisher-specific layouts and richer extraction targets such as metadata, images, and tables. Direct LLM-based extraction offers greater flexibility, but incurs substantial cost and latency at scale, while manually engineered publisher-specific parsers can achieve high accuracy but require substantial human effort to build and maintain.
  We introduce PACE, an agentic framework for learning publisher-specific extraction configurations from representative pages and user requirements. During training, PACE uses LLMs to analyze page structure and aggregate reusable extraction patterns. At inference time, the learned configurations instantiate a fixed deterministic extractor template, enabling scalable extraction without additional LLM calls.
  Experiments spanning article-body, metadata, and multimodal extraction show that PACE outperforms scalable non-manual baselines while approaching the quality of manually engineered publisher-specific parsers. PACE achieves stronger extraction of article text, metadata, images, and tables, demonstrating that agentic configuration learning can automate publisher-specific extraction for LLM-ready page representations beyond article text.

</details>


### [81] [UIC-AIHealth4All at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Answer-First Evidence Grounding for Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27467)
*Mohammad Arvan,Hossein Haeri,Natalie Parde,Rebecca T. Feinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UIC-AIHealth4All系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中的表现，参与子任务2（证据识别）、3（答案生成）和4（答案-证据对齐）。针对证据识别和答案生成提出"答案优先"流水线，即先让模型生成引用具体笔记句子的候选答案，再判断完整证据集；针对子任务4采用五次独立模型调用的自洽投票机制按阈值保留链接。在三个子任务中分别排名第三（Strict Micro F1 62.90）、第九（Overall 31.90）和第五（F1 79.81）。事后语言学分析发现，尽管模型输出的词数和句数与临床医师撰写的参考答案匹配，但可读性仍高出3.2个Flesch-Kincaid年级水平，表明临床NLP系统需要显式优化可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的接地问答需要准确识别证据、生成可靠的答案并建立答案与证据之间的对齐关系。作者认为，在抽象层面判断句子相关性与基于生成答案判断其相关性存在不对称性，因此提出先生成答案再判定证据集的策略，以更好地利用这一不对称性；同时探索自洽投票以提升对齐任务的鲁棒性。

Method: 系统采用"答案优先"流水线：对于子任务2和3，模型首先生成引用特定笔记句子的候选答案，然后再进行完整证据集的分类判定。对于子任务4，通过五次独立模型调用执行自洽性投票，依据投票阈值保留答案-证据链接。事后分析采用45个文体特征对模型输出与临床医师参考文本进行可读性对比，使用Flesch-Kincaid年级水平等指标。

Result: 系统在证据识别子任务上排名第三（Strict Micro F1 62.90），答案生成子任务上排名第九（Overall 31.90），答案-证据对齐子任务上排名第五（F1 79.81）。语言学分析显示模型输出与医师参考词数和句数匹配，但可读性仍高出3.2个Flesch-Kincaid年级水平。

Conclusion: "答案优先"流水线在证据识别任务上表现突出，自洽投票在对齐任务上取得了不错的成绩。但模型输出的可读性与临床医师存在显著差距，说明在临床NLP系统中可读性应作为显式优化目标而非默认能力。代码和提示词已开源。

Abstract: We describe the UIC-AIHealth4All system for ArchEHR-QA 2026, a shared task on grounded question answering from electronic health records. We participated in Subtasks 2 (evidence identification), 3 (answer generation), and 4 (answer-evidence alignment). For Subtasks 2 and 3, we propose an answer-first pipeline in which the model generates candidate answers citing specific note sentences before classifying the full evidence set, exploiting the asymmetry between judging relevance in the abstract versus relative to a generated answer. For Subtask 4, we apply self-consistency voting over five independent model calls, retaining links by vote threshold. Our pipeline ranked third on evidence identification (Strict Micro F1 62.90), ninth on answer generation (Overall 31.90), and fifth on answer-evidence alignment (F1 79.81). A post-hoc linguistic analysis of 45 stylistic features reveals that model outputs remain 3.2 Flesch-Kincaid grade levels harder to read than clinician-authored references despite matching their word and sentence counts, suggesting readability warrants explicit optimization in clinical NLP systems. Code and prompts are available at https://github.com/mo-arvan/archehr-qa-2026-uic-aihealth4all.

</details>


### [82] [Select, Don't Train: The Benefits of Modular Entity Disambiguation with LLM-Based Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27470)
*Fina Polat,Daniel Daza,Pengyu Zhang,Klim Zaporojets,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在LLM选择器下不同检索策略（BM25、Web KB搜索、训练密集检索器）的实体消歧效果，发现将选择任务委托给强大的LLM后，训练检索器带来的增益有限；无训练的BM25+LLM管线在ZELDA基准上达到SOTA（86.3 inKB micro-F1），并首次引入对检索失败时弃权的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体消歧（ED）系统通常将候选实体检索与正确实体选择作为单一任务在共享嵌入空间中联合优化，导致检索召回与精细选择之间难以兼顾；且训练检索器随知识图谱动态变化维护成本高昂。尽管已有工作尝试将检索器与LLM选择器结合，但两阶段间的相互作用尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在共享LLM选择阶段下，系统比较三种候选生成检索策略：稀疏检索BM25、Web KB搜索、以及SOTA训练密集检索器，并搭配多种开源与闭源LLM作为选择器。同时，提出在检测到检索失败（正确实体不在候选中）时允许弃权的评估框架。

Result: 将选择委托给强大的LLM后，训练检索器仅带来适度增益：无训练的BM25+LLM在ZELDA上实现SOTA（inKB micro-F1从82.3提升到86.3，+4点）；相同LLM配合训练密集检索器达到88.5。在奖励正确弃权的评估设置下，BM25+LLM管线达到90.7 F1。

Conclusion: 解耦检索与选择阶段是可行的，且在LLM具备强选择能力时，简化的无训练检索管线即可达到最佳或接近最佳效果。此外，解耦框架天然支持弃权机制，提升系统在处理无正确答案场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Entity Disambiguation (ED) is a key task for constructing and using knowledge graphs. State-of-the-art neural approaches commonly model ED as a single task, although it consists of two distinct subproblems: retrieving candidate entities and selecting the correct one given context. Dual-encoder models optimize for both within a shared embedding space, forcing representations to balance high-recall retrieval with fine-grained selection, and they require trained retrievers, which are costly to maintain as knowledge graphs change. While recent work has begun to combine retrievers with LLM-based selectors, the interplay between the two stages has not been studied systematically. In this paper, we present a systematic comparison of retrieval strategies for candidate generation under a shared LLM-based selection stage, combining sparse retrieval (BM25), Web KB search, and a state-of-the-art trained dense retriever with several open- and closed-source LLMs. We show that, once selection is delegated to a capable LLM, training the retriever provides only modest additional value: a fully training-free BM25 retriever paired with an LLM selector reaches a new state of the art on the ZELDA benchmark, raising inKB micro-F1 from 82.3 to 86.3 (+4); pairing the same LLM with a trained dense retriever reaches 88.5. Decoupling retrieval from selection also exposes a limitation of current ED systems: when the correct entity is missing from retrieved candidates, they are forced to predict an incorrect entity. In contrast, our framework allows for abstention when retrieval failure is detected. In an evaluation setting that rewards correct abstentions, the training-free BM25 + LLM pipeline reaches 90.7 F1.

</details>


### [83] [XHotpotQA: A Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Knowledge Composition in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27481)
*Iman Barati,Arash Ghafouri,Behrouz Minaei-Bidgoli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍XHotpotQA，一个用于跨语言知识组合的多跳问答基准。与现有基准简单翻译整个示例不同，XHotpotQA显式分配问题、桥接证据、答案承载证据和干扰项的语言角色，以诊断推理链中跨语言边界的失败。包含15,661个训练集和7,405个验证集实例，实验发现完整问题-证据语言不匹配导致F1下降10.25-15.79点，不同文字证据导致11.98-23.70点缺陷，且读者模型的条件相关缺陷显著大于选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言多跳问答基准通常将整个示例统一翻译成一种语言，这掩盖了推理链内部语言边界处的失败模式。需要一种受控基准来显式测试系统在混合语言证据上进行知识组合的能力。

Method: 构建XHotpotQA基准：将每个实例建模为证据依赖图，其中问题、桥接证据、答案承载证据和干扰项具有显式语言分配。提供句子级支持监督和干扰项，含15,661个训练实例和7,405个验证实例。通过三种读者工件和适应选择器的对比实验，评估在不同语言对齐条件下的性能差异。

Result: 验证集中99.81%的实例跨越问题到黄金证据的语言界面，95.60%使用不同语言的黄金段落。完整问题-证据不匹配使答案F1下降10.25-15.79点，不同文字证据导致11.98-23.70点缺陷；适应选择器的对应对比仅为1.71和1.78点，表明读者模型的条件相关缺陷远大于选择器。

Conclusion: XHotpotQA提供了角色感知诊断、模块化评估和经过审计的测试平台，用于评估必须跨语言整合证据的知识型系统。在给定候选集的设计下，读者模型比选择器表现出更大的语言不匹配缺陷。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive multi-hop question answering requires systems to select evidence and compose dependent facts, yet multilingual benchmarks usually translate an entire example into one language. This hides failures at language boundaries inside the reasoning chain. We introduce XHotpotQA, a controlled benchmark for cross-lingual knowledge composition over mixed-language evidence. Each instance is modeled as an evidence-dependency graph whose question, bridge evidence, answer-bearing evidence, and distractors have explicit language assignments. The audited resource contains 15,661 training and 7,405 validation instances, with sentence-level support supervision and supplied distractors. In validation, 99.81% of items cross the question-to-gold-evidence language interface and 95.60% use gold paragraphs in different languages. Across three reader artifacts, full question-evidence mismatch is associated with 10.25 to 15.79 lower Unicode-aware answer F1 than partial alignment, and different-script evidence with deficits of 11.98 to 23.70 points; the corresponding adapted-selector contrasts are 1.71 and 1.78 points. Under this supplied-candidate design, the evaluated readers therefore show substantially larger condition-associated deficits than the selector. XHotpotQA provides role-aware diagnostics, modular evaluation, and an audited test bed for knowledge-based systems that must integrate evidence across languages.

</details>


### [84] [INSPIRE: An Internalize-Then-Improve Approach for Example-Driven Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27501)
*Shuai Wang,Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Haojing Huang,Xinnian Liang,Ying Shen,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出INSPIRE框架，通过Reference-Guided Student Internalization（RGSI）和分阶段rubric偏好训练策略，增强LLM的例证推理（构造反例）能力，在多模型规模上实现一致提升且不损害通用数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数学推理优化方法主要关注最终答案正确性，忽略了模型是否真正内化数学概念。人类数学教育中通过构造反例测试定理边界的例证推理反映了深层概念理解，但当前LLM在此能力上发展不足。同时，通过偏好优化增强该能力面临两大挑战：模型自身例证推理能力有限导致难以构造有效偏好对；能力习得是渐进式的，需先学会采用该策略再学会正确应用。

Method: 提出INSPIRE（Internalize-Then-Improve）方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) Reference-Guided Student Internalization (RGSI)，在策略模型自身分布下生成高质量偏好候选；(2) 分阶段rubric偏好训练策略，将学习过程分解为方法导向（method-oriented）和正确性导向（correctness-oriented）两个阶段。

Result: 在多种模型规模和模型家族上的实验均展示了一致的性能提升，甚至超越更大规模的开源模型；在分布外基准上的评估确认通用数学推理能力没有退化。

Conclusion: INSPIRE通过"内化-再改进"的递进式训练策略，有效增强了LLM构造反例的例证推理能力，验证了先学习策略再学习正确应用的渐进式能力习得假设，且在不牺牲通用数学推理能力的前提下实现跨规模泛化。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has seen rapid progress in large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods optimize predominantly for final-answer correctness, raising the question whether models truly internalize mathematical concepts or merely memorize solution patterns. In human mathematics education, example-based reasoning such as constructing counterexamples to test theorem boundaries reflects deep conceptual understanding, but remains underdeveloped in current LLMs. Enhancing this capability through preference optimization presents two key challenges: (1) the model's limited example-based reasoning ability makes constructing effective preference pairs inherently difficult; and (2) capability acquisition is progressive, as the model must first learn to adopt this strategy before learning to apply it correctly. Therefore we propose INSPIRE, an Internalize-Then-Improve approach combining Reference-Guided Student Internalization (RGSI), which produces high-quality preference candidates under the policy model's own distribution, with a stage-wise rubric preference training strategy that decomposes learning into method-oriented and correctness-oriented stages. Experiments across multiple model scales and families demonstrate consistent improvements, even surpassing larger open-source models, while evaluations on out-of-distribution benchmarks confirm no degradation in general mathematical reasoning ability.

</details>


### [85] [A Survey on Rubric-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27505)
*Zifei Shan,Fangning Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"rubric引导的强化学习"（Rubric-guided RL）的综述，提出贝叶斯框架将宪法定义为评估标准的先验分布 P(R)，将rubric定义为条件实例化 R_x ~ P(R|x)，并沿先验-后验轴对宪法AI、实例特定rubric、过程级监督、自进化rubric及其智能体和多模态扩展进行分类，同时从语言学视角分析了粒度权衡、语义漂移和语言奖励黑客对对齐可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLHF依赖标量奖励信号，缺乏可解释性且无法捕捉响应质量的多面性。文章旨在通过引入结构化、可解释的评估标准（rubrics）作为奖励设计、反馈生成和策略优化的支柱，解决这一局限性，并提供一个统一的框架来梳理该领域日益增长的文献。

Method: 提出了一个贝叶斯统一框架：将宪法/宪法型原则定义为评估标准的先验分布 P(R)，将具体rubric定义为给定上下文条件 x 下的条件实例化 R_x ~ P(R|x)。基于这一"先验-后验"轴，构建了包含宪法AI、实例特定rubrics、过程级监督、自进化rubrics以及智能体和多模态扩展的分类体系。此外，从语言学角度对rubric作为自然语言制品进行了分析，考察粒度权衡（granularity trade-offs）、语义漂移（semantic drift）和语言奖励黑客（linguistic reward hacking）对对齐可靠性的影响。

Result: 文章提出了一个统一的贝叶斯分类框架来组织rubric引导的RL研究，覆盖多个前沿方向（宪法AI、过程级监督、自进化rubrics、智能体和多模态扩展），并通过语言学分析识别了影响对齐可靠性的关键挑战，包括粒度权衡、语义漂移和语言奖励黑客等开放问题。

Conclusion: 文章指出rubric引导的RL作为传统RLHF的改进方向具有广阔前景，但自然语言形态的rubrics在语义稳定性、粒度选择和奖励安全性方面仍面临重要开放问题，这些问题是未来研究的重点方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has become the dominant paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, traditional RLHF relies on scalar reward signals that lack interpretability and fail to capture the multifaceted nature of response quality. Rubric-guided reinforcement learning addresses these limitations by introducing structured, interpretable evaluation criteria, or rubrics, as the backbone of reward design, feedback generation, and policy optimization. In this survey, we introduce a Bayesian framework that defines constitutions as prior distributions $P(R)$ over evaluation criteria and rubrics as conditional instantiations $R_x \sim P(R|x)$. Under this unified view, we present a taxonomy of rubric-guided RL along the prior-posterior axis, covering constitutional AI, instance-specific rubrics, process-level supervision, self-evolving rubrics, and their agentic and multimodal extensions. Furthermore, as rubrics are natural-language artifacts, we present a linguistic analysis of how granularity trade-offs, semantic drift, and linguistic reward hacking impact alignment reliability, identifying key open problems for future research.

</details>


### [86] [How Do Linear Probes Emerge? A Circuit-Tracing Framework with Concept-Targeted Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27510)
*Vedant Palit,Florent Draye,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念定向归因（CTA）框架，训练以线性探针方向为目标的归因图，而非传统的下一token概率目标。通过跨层转码器（Cross-Layer Transcoders），CTA能揭示与探针预测性能相关的可解释电路结构，区分探针目标与logit目标电路的功能差异，实现从行为层面探针准确率到机制层面解释的跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transcoder归因图仅解释模型为何对某个next token赋予高概率，但无法解释内部概念表征（由线性探针测量）为何在提示词中出现。需要一种框架将探针性能与可解释的电路结构联系起来，从而回答不仅是探针是否有效，还包括哪些内部计算使其有效，这对安全关键概念审计尤为重要。

Method: 1) 引入CTA：以线性探针方向为训练目标构建归因图（区别于logit目标）；2) 使用Cross-Layer Transcoders生成探针定向的电路；3) 在图级别和局部特征层面提取预测性结构；4) 通过因果消融实验对比探针目标与logit目标电路在功能上的差异。

Result: 图级特征可预测四个广泛研究概念类别上的探针准确率（ρ=0.91，R²=0.84）；局部特征可识别驱动逐提示词分类的稀疏组件。因果消融显示：移除探针相关特征降低内部概念分数但基本保留生成token，而移除logit相关特征在92%-100%的情况下改变生成token且对探针分数影响接近零。

Conclusion: CTA为探针性能提供了从行为精度到机制解释的过渡框架，使探针目标电路与logit目标电路具有功能上截然不同的机制，支持对内部概念表征（包括安全关键概念）进行更细粒度的审计。

Abstract: Transcoder attribution graphs are usually trained to explain why a model assigns high probability to a particular next token. We introduce Concept-Targeted Attribution (CTA), which instead trains attribution graphs with respect to a linear probe direction. CTA therefore yields probe-specific circuits that explain why an internal concept representation arises in a prompt, independently of whether it is expressed in the generated token. Using Cross-Layer Transcoders, we show that these probe-targeted graphs contain predictive structure: graph-level features predict probe accuracy across four widely studied concept categories ($ρ= 0.91$, $R^2 = 0.84$), while local features identify the sparse components driving per-prompt classification. This connects probe performance to interpretable circuit structure, allowing us to ask not only whether a probe works, but which internal computations make it work. Causal ablations further show that probe-targeted and logit-targeted graphs capture functionally distinct mechanisms. Removing probe-relevant features reduces internal concept scores while largely preserving generated tokens, whereas removing logit-relevant features changes the generated token in 92% to 100% of cases with near-zero effect on probe scores. CTA provides a framework for moving from behavioral probe accuracy to mechanistic explanations of probe performance, enabling more detailed audits of internal concept representations, including safety-critical ones. Our code is available at https://github.com/vedantpalit/concept-targeted-attribution

</details>


### [87] [Trajectory-Level Speculative Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27514)
*Tianxiang Pan,Baitao Gong,Mo Guang,Hongwei Yong,Tianpeng Jiang,Yaqian Li,Zheng Cao,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轨迹级投机解码框架，针对扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的并行解码难题，通过置信度分层树探索构建草稿去噪轨迹，并用块级并行评估和双向掩码验证，实现7-14倍加速和每步token数从2.6提升到4.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的解码策略在低置信度下会退化为单token生成，严重限制了吞吐量。与自回归模型中从左到右的序列投机解码不同，dLLMs需要对去噪轨迹（包含显式位置和掩码顺序的多token更新序列）进行投机，这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出轨迹级投机框架：通过置信度分层树探索构建草稿去噪轨迹；利用块级并行评估和双向注意力掩码进行验证；引入块间投机机制，利用扩散模型的双向结构进行跨块前瞻；并基于Fast-dLLM的双缓存基础设施实现。

Result: 去噪迭代减少30-40%，每步token数从2.6提升到4.3，相对朴素dLLMs实现7-14倍加速，相对Fast-dLLM实现1.3倍加速，在推理和代码基准上精度变化小于1%。

Conclusion: 作者正式刻画了该方法的精确性条件，识别出轨迹漂移是增加并行度的根本代价。实验表明轨迹级投机解码能有效利用扩散模型的并行潜力，在保持精度的同时大幅提升dLLMs的推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based language models (dLLMs) enable parallel token generation through iterative denoising, but existing decoding strategies collapse to single-token generation under low confidence, severely limiting throughput. Unlike autoregressive models where speculative decoding operates on token sequences in a fixed left-to-right order, dLLMs require speculating over denoising trajectories-sequences of multi-token updates with explicit positions and unmasking orders. We develop a trajectory-level speculative framework that constructs draft denoising trajectories via confidence-stratified tree exploration and verifies them through blockwise parallel evaluation with bidirectional attention masking. Our method further introduces inter-block speculation, exploiting diffusion models' bidirectional structure to perform cross-block lookahead. We formally characterize when this approach is exact and identify trajectory drift as the fundamental cost of increased parallelism. Building on Fast-dLLM's dual-cache infrastructure, our framework reduces denoising iterations by 30-40% and increases tokens-per-step from 2.6 to 4.3, achieving 7-14x speedup over vanilla dLLMs and 1.3x over Fast-dLLM with less than 1% accuracy change across reasoning and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [88] [When Tokenizers Fail: Byte-Level Chunking for Zero-Shot Transfer to Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27658)
*Sanjeev Kumar,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种适配的分层字节级网络框架，直接从冻结子词模型的表示中初始化字节嵌入，通过块对齐损失和轻量级词性标注监督来弥合字节级与子词级表示之间的模态鸿沟，无需大规模训练即可提升低资源语言的词级形态学任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子词分词将主导语言的频率模式强加给共享脚本的语言，不利于低资源语言处理；字节级模型虽绕过此问题，但在非拉丁字母脚本的词级任务上存在粒度不匹配；现有分层字节级架构需要大量训练数据，且与冻结的子词语言模型配对时存在表示不对齐的问题。

Method: 提出一种适配的分层网络框架：1) 直接从冻结基座模型的子词表示中初始化字节嵌入，弥合模态差距；2) 应用块对齐损失，将动态分组的字节块投射到预计算的子词目标上；3) 穿插轻量级的词性标注监督以引导边界检测。

Result: 在六种语言上的实验表明，该方法无需分词器的方案在词级形态学任务上表现更优，词性标注任务最高提升13.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架以轻量方式克服了字节级架构与冻结子词模型之间的表示不对齐问题，在低资源语言上实现了无需繁重训练的词级任务性能提升。

Abstract: Subword tokenization hinders low-resource language processing by imposing frequency patterns from dominant languages onto script-sharing variants. Byte-level models bypass this issue by processing raw UTF-8 characters, yet they create a granularity mismatch for word-level tasks in non-Latin scripts. Hierarchical byte-level architectures address this mismatch by grouping bytes into word-aligned chunks. However, these architectures require massive training data and suffer from representational misalignment when paired with frozen subword-based language models. In this paper, we propose an adapted hierarchical network framework that bridges this modality gap without extensive training. Our method initializes byte embeddings directly from the subword representations of a frozen base model. We apply a chunk alignment loss to project dynamically grouped byte chunks toward precomputed subword targets, and interleave lightweight part-of-speech (POS) supervision to guide boundary detection. Experiments across six languages demonstrate that our tokenizer-free approach improves performance for word-level morphological tasks, yielding up to a 13.3% improvement on POS tagging.

</details>


### [89] [Knowing Before Answering: Decoding Language Models for Reliable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27661)
*Syed Mahbubul Huq,Christopher Child,Tillman Weyde,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用语言模型内部信号（隐藏激活和注意力特征）训练轻量级线性分类器，用于RAG场景中判断检索文档是否充足、不足或存在冲突，在16个不同架构和规模的语言模型上均优于提示词基线和专门的RAG模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在检索增强生成（RAG）中，检索到的文档可能不足以回答问题或包含相互冲突的信息，系统需要能够识别这些情况，而不仅仅是决定何时回答。现有方法缺乏对检索信息质量（充足、不足、冲突）的系统性判别。

Method: 将问题构建为三分类任务（充足/不足/冲突）。创建了包含虚构信息的受控基准数据集，复现RAG场景并标注类别。使用模型隐藏激活和注意力派生特征作为输入，训练轻量级线性模型进行三分类。

Result: 在16种涵盖不同架构和模型规模的语言模型上，基于特征的路由器一致优于基于提示的基线和专门的RAG模型。分析发现：最有信息的信号位于中间层；在大多数测试模型中，隐藏激活状态比注意力值和MLP特征输出更有效。

Conclusion: 语言模型内部编码了检索证据是否足以支持回答的信息，这一信号可以可靠地被解码用于RAG分流（triage）决策。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval may provide insufficient or conflicting information needed to answer a question. The system should not only know when to answer but also be able to identify cases in which the documents provided in RAG are insufficient or contain conflicting information. This can be framed as a three-way classification problem, where we use the model's internal signals to determine whether the provided information in the input can be classified as sufficient, insufficient, or conflicting. We create a controlled benchmark dataset that replicates a RAG setup with fictitious information and labels each instance as answerable, insufficient, or conflicting. We use hidden activations and attention-derived features as inputs to train a lightweight linear model to distinguish among the three classes. Across 16 language models spanning different architectures and a range of model sizes, our feature-based router consistently outperforms prompting-based baselines and the performance of specialised RAG-models. We further conduct analyses into the information dynamics of the models. We show that the most informative signals for the classification are available in the middle layers, with hidden activation states being more effective than attention values or the MLP-feature outputs in most of the tested models. Overall, our results suggest that language models internally encode whether retrieved evidence is sufficient to support answering, and that this signal can be decoded reliably for RAG triage.

</details>


### [90] [First Make It Playable, Then Make It Good: Staged Interaction Learning for Small Dialogue-Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27672)
*Syed Mahbubul Huq,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-GuidePlay-2B，一个仅2B参数规模的对话游戏交互语言模型，通过三步微调策略（成功轨迹SFT、加权轮次级SFT、教师引导SFT）在Playpen验证集上达到57.12 clemscore，并在官方挑战中获得第二高的clemscore增量（相对基座模型提升约+36）。研究发现模仿完整轨迹有助于可玩性，轮次级和教师引导训练提升决策能力，而重放修复和困难样本挖掘等复杂策略无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小规模语言模型（2B参数）在对话游戏交互任务上的表现，验证通过简单有效的数据策展策略而非复杂的程序化方法即可让小型模型具备竞争力。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5-2B进行三步微调：(a) 仅在Playpen成功游戏轨迹上进行SFT；(b) 加权轮次级SFT（turn-level weighted SFT）；(c) 教师引导SFT（teacher-guided SFT），其中教师模型（更大规模）仅用于修复格式和评估样本质量，不生成新的gold action。

Result: 在公开Playpen验证集上达到57.12 clemscore和42.68 statscore；在官方挑战结果中，Playpen clemscore增量排名第二（相对基座模型提升约+36）。

Conclusion: 模仿完整轨迹可提升可玩性；轮次级和教师引导训练能改善决策并提高总分。重放修复和困难样本挖掘等程序化重型方法无帮助，说明小型模型通过精心策展即可高效达成性能，无需激进策略。模型和代码已开源。

Abstract: We present Qwen-GuidePlay-2B, a 2B-parameter language model for dialogue-game interaction. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-2B using three steps: a) SFT on only successful game trajectories from Playpen, b) weighted turn-level SFT, and c) teacher-guided SFT. The teacher model (which is a larger model) is only used to fix formatting and evaluate examples, but does not create new gold actions. Our final model scores 57.12 clemscore and 42.68 statscore on the public Playpen validation. In the officially released challenge results, our model obtains the second-highest Playpen clemscore delta among submitted systems (which is approximately +36 over its base model). Our findings suggest that imitating full trajectories helps with playability, while turn-level and teacher-guided training usually improve decision-making and increase the overall score. Alternative procedurally heavy approaches like replay-repair and hard-example mining did not help, which suggests that small models are performant simply by using careful curation strategies rather than aggressive changes. We make available both the model and the code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [91] [Below the Noise Floor: Bimodal Seed Collapse and Distinct Failure Modes in Small-Model Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27729)
*Dipto Sumit,Sakib Ul Haque,Farig Sadeque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型学生模型在医疗API路由任务中进行知识蒸馏（KD）时的种子方差和崩溃模式问题。在740实例的医疗API路由任务上（1.5B Qwen学生、20B教师），通过多种子（3-6个）对比8种KD变体和监督交叉熵，发现单种子评估无法检测小型模型KD中的关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏文献多在单种子设置下报告增益，种子方差未知。在函数路由部署场景中，小型学生模型具有吸引力，但其KD训练可能存在未检测的稳定性问题，需要系统性地评估种子方差对KD增益可靠性的影响。

Method: 在740实例的医疗API路由任务上，使用1.5B Qwen学生模型和20B教师模型，对比8种知识蒸馏变体与监督交叉熵基线。对关键配置使用3至6个随机种子重复实验，测量种子标准差并检测崩溃模式。

Result: （i）每个种子的标准差为2.8至48.7个百分点，吞没了所有低于5个百分点的KD增益；（ii）7种KD变体中有3种表现出双峰崩溃，至少1/3至1/5的种子低于55%准确率，另有1种方差升高；（iii）崩溃模式不同——ce_kd和ce_paraphrase出现错误函数选择崩溃，reasoning_kd出现此前未记录的输出截断崩溃（0.9%准确率）；（iv）仅progressive_kd和rank_kd未观察崩溃，标准差≤3.9个百分点；（v）输入增强的跨分裂+3.78pp增益在受控同分裂多种子重测后反转为-2.70pp。

Conclusion: 单种子评估无法可靠检测小型学生模型知识蒸馏中的关键失败模式。知识蒸馏研究必须采用多种子评估，至少需要足以覆盖种子方差的重复实验，才能保证报告增益的可信度。

Abstract: Function routing -- selecting the correct API call from a fixed catalog given a natural-language request -- is a deployment problem where small students are attractive but knowledge distillation gains are typically reported single-seed, at scales where seed variance is unknown. On a 740-instance healthcare API routing task with a 1.5B Qwen student and a 20B teacher, we compare eight KD variants against supervised cross-entropy, using three to six seeds for key configurations. We find: (i) per-seed standard deviation ranges from 2.8 to 48.7 percentage points, swallowing every claimed KD gain below five points; (ii) three of seven KD variants exhibit bimodal collapse, with at least one in three to five seeds falling below 55% accuracy while the others train normally, and a fourth showing elevated variance; (iii) collapse has distinct modes -- wrong-function selection for ce_kd and ce_paraphrase, and a previously undocumented output-truncation mode for reasoning_kd, where the model emits reasoning but terminates before producing a function name (0.9% accuracy); (iv) only progressive_kd and rank_kd avoid collapse across observed seeds, with sigma <= 3.9 pp; (v) a naive cross-split +3.78 pp gain from input enrichment reverses to -2.70 pp under controlled within-split multi-seed re-testing. Single-seed evaluation is therefore unable to detect central failure modes in small-model KD.

</details>


### [92] [Load-Bearing Context: The Question Damage Score for Evaluating Context Reliance in Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27756)
*Neh Majmudar,Elena Filatova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断框架，利用自我包含的语言学奥林匹克谜题来分析大型语言模型是依赖上下文还是先验知识进行回答。通过删除单个上下文示例（随机或针对性删除），并引入"问题损伤分数"来量化影响，发现前沿LLM在关键信息被移除后很少选择弃权，反而继续给出正确答案，揭示了模型对上下文依赖的复杂模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断大语言模型是从上下文中推导答案还是依赖先验知识仍然是一个根本性挑战。需要一种受控的实验设置来隔离这两种可能的信息来源，从而更好地理解模型的行为机制。

Method: 利用53个英国语言学奥林匹克谜题（自我包含、所有答案仅来源于专家设计的上下文示例），生成两个修改版本：(1) 均匀随机删除单个上下文示例，(2) 受纠错码启发的针对性删除以移除承载结构性信息的关键示例。引入问题损伤分数（Question Damage Score）将谜题分类为脆弱或鲁棒，并在指示模型信息不足时应弃权的条件下评估三个前沿LLM。

Result: 发现模型在被移除承重上下文后很少选择弃权，仍然继续输出正确答案。这表明模型可能依赖先验知识、记忆或上下文之外的推理机制来填补信息缺口，而非严格依赖给定的上下文。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能检测弃权行为，还能支持更细粒度的上下文依赖分析，包括因果干预、停止集分析、定向污染研究和机制可解释性。这些发现为进一步研究基于上下文的推理、先验知识、记忆和语言推理提供了动机和工具基础。

Abstract: Determining whether large language models derive answers from context or prior knowledge remains a fundamental challenge. Self-contained linguistic olympiad puzzles provide a controlled setting where all answers derive solely from expert-designed context examples without external knowledge. Removing individual context examples can eliminate information needed for specific questions while leaving the rest of the puzzle unchanged. We leverage this to introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing individual context examples. Using 53 UK Linguistics Olympiad puzzles, we generate two modified variants by deleting a single context example: (1) uniform random deletion, and (2) targeted deletion (inspired by error-correcting codes) to remove a structurally load-bearing example uniquely carrying necessary information. We formalize this impact using a Question Damage Score to classify puzzles as fragile or robust. Evaluating three frontier LLMs under instructions to abstain when information is insufficient, we find they rarely abstain, often continuing to produce correct answers after load-bearing context is removed. These findings motivate further investigation into context-based reasoning, prior knowledge, memorization, and linguistic inference. Beyond abstention, the framework enables fine-grained analyses of context reliance, including causal interventions, stopping-set analysis, targeted contamination studies, and mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [93] [Informational Antilocality and the Locality Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27760)
*Andrew McInnerney,Shane Storks,Steven Abney,Richard L. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Transformer的语言模型（LLM）学习"k-反局部语言"（即任意k个连续符号跨度内无互信息的语言）的能力。研究者构造了不同k值的此类语言，发现LLM在不同反局部程度下能达到相近的交叉熵损失，但在更反局部的语言上收敛更慢。这支持了"非局部依赖更难学习"的观点，但证据来自学习速度而非最终学习成功度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Transformer语言模型在缺乏局部统计规律（互信息）的语言上是否能有效学习，以检验语言模型对局部依赖的归纳偏置是否影响其学习非局部结构的能力。

Method: 构造k-反局部语言（任意k个连续符号跨度内无互信息），通过增大k值逐步消除局部依赖，训练LLM并比较其交叉熵损失和学习收敛速度。

Result: LLM在反局部语言上能达到与局部语言相当的交叉熵损失，但在更反局部（更大的k）的语言上收敛速度明显变慢。

Conclusion: 非局部依赖确实更难学习，但这一偏置体现在学习速率而非最终学习效果上——LLM最终仍能充分学习反局部语言，只是需要更多训练时间。

Abstract: We consider the ability of transformer-based language models (LLMs) to learn what we call k-antilocal languages, i.e., languages that have no mutual information across any span of $k$ contiguous symbols. We construct such languages with increasing $k$, finding that LLMs trained on them achieve comparable cross-entropy loss regardless of antilocality, but converge more slowly on more antilocal languages. Our findings support the idea that non-local dependencies are more difficult to learn, but the evidence for this bias comes from learning speed rather than learning success.

</details>


### [94] [Compositional Failure in Audio-Visual LLMs: Late-Layer Prior Dominance Under Cross-modal Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27785)
*Adarsh Sudheer,David Li,Omar Elbanna,Ishaan Kodarapu,Arjun Bahuguna,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究音视频冲突（audio-visual conflict）作为音频-视频大语言模型（AV-LLMs）的组合泛化测试。研究发现模型在遇到语义不兼容但时间同步的音视频对时，表现出"先验主导"（prior dominance）的失败模式：模型在深层网络中过早承诺于内部偏好的答案模式，而非基于冲突输入进行推理。机制可解释性分析表明这种承诺集中在约25.5±1层，且增强时间对齐只能改变答案偏差，无法改善组合冲突解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV-LLMs在多模态理解中表现优异，但其组合泛化能力（即同时处理来自不同模态的冲突信息并做出正确判断）尚未被充分测试。现有基准如AVHBench主要测试模型在一致或不一致数据上的表现，但缺乏对模型如何应对"同步但语义冲突"的音频-视觉对的研究。理解这种失败机制对于改进模型架构和训练策略至关重要。

Method: 1) 将音视频冲突作为组合泛化测试任务，要求模型结合时间同步但语义不兼容的音视频证据判断是否匹配。2) 在VideoLLaMA 2-7B-AV上测试三种对齐配置，并在AVHBench的精确字符串Yes/No子集上评估。3) 使用InternVideo2进行跨模态冲突的离架测试。4) 通过机制可解释性分析（mechanistic interpretability）定位模型内部"承诺"（commitment）发生的层数。

Result: 1) VideoLLaMA 2-7B-AV的三种对齐配置在AVHBench的Yes/No子集上保持接近随机水平，尽管其输出先验发生了显著偏移。2) InternVideo2在跨模态冲突下出现32.3%的准确率下降和17.3%的指令跟随失败。3) 机制分析发现模型对答案的"承诺"集中在25.5±1层。4) 增强时间对齐能改变答案偏差，但不能改善组合冲突解决能力。

Conclusion: AV-LLMs存在"先验主导"的失败模式：模型晚期层会执着于一个在冲突输入中缺乏充分依据的内部偏好答案模式，导致在组合冲突场景下无法正确推理。这揭示了当前AV-LLMs在组合泛化方面的根本局限，即使增强时间对齐或调整输出先验也无法根本解决。作者公开了代码和数据以支持进一步研究和可复现的机制审计。

Abstract: We study audio-visual conflict as a compositional generalization test for AV-LLMs: the model must combine synchronized but semantically incompatible audio and video evidence and decide whether the pair matches. On VideoLLaMA 2-7B-AV, three alignment configurations remain nearchance on the scored exact-string Yes/No subset of AVHBench, even though their output priors shift substantially. Similarly, off-the-shelf InternVideo2 experienced a 32.3% accuracy decrease specifically under cross-modal conflict, accompanied by a 17.3% instruction-following failure. We call this failure mode prior dominance: late-layer commitment to an internally preferred answer pattern that is weakly grounded in the conflicting inputs. To explain this behavior, we conduct a mechanistic interpretability analysis and find that commitment remains concentrated at 25.5 $\pm$ 1 layers. We show that stronger temporal alignment changes answer bias, but do not improve compositional conflict resolution. Code and data to reproduce our mechanistic audit and behavioral evaluations are available at https://github.com/AdarshSudheer09/AVHBench-dmai.

</details>


### [95] [Representation of syntax in LLMs through the lens of linear distance and similarity-aware entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27813)
*Juan Pablo Vigneaux,Mary Kennedy,Khalil Iskarous,Robert Frank,Matilde Marcolli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对结构探针（Structural Probes）的评估指标进行细粒度分解，提出按句法标签分类的无向依存分数（UASL），并发现两个因素（词线性距离的均值与离散度、句法关系中心词的多样性）能解释大部分UASL的变异，揭示语言模型中句法表示的抽象程度及对嵌入空间几何性质的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构探针评估只使用整体无向无标签依存分数（UUAS），掩盖了不同句法关系在重建准确率上的差异。作者希望细粒度地分解这一指标，找出决定不同句法关系重建难度的关键因素，从而更深入地理解语言模型如何抽象地表征句法结构。

Method: 将整体UUAS细分为按句法标签计算的无向附着分数（UASL），分别评估每种句法关系的重建准确率；随后通过回归分析识别两个预测因素：(i) 相关词之间线性距离（对数尺度）的均值和离散度；(ii) 句法关系中心词的多样性（基于相似度的熵）。在多种模型规模和架构上进行验证。

Result: 发现不同句法关系在重建准确率上存在显著差异，这些差异与语言学的分类相重叠；词间线性距离的均值和离散度以及中心词多样性这两个因素能预测大部分UASL的变异，该结论在多种模型规模和架构上保持一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了语言模型中句法表示的抽象程度——句法关系重建的难易与嵌入空间的几何性质（如词间距离的分布）密切相关，说明句法表示并非完全抽象，而是受限于嵌入空间的几何结构。

Abstract: Structural probes were introduced by Hewitt and Manning to reconstruct syntactic trees from a neural language model's latent representations. They are evaluated by calculating the proportion of syntactic tree edges correctly reconstructed over an annotated corpus (as measured by undirected unlabeled attachment score). Here, we disaggregate this measure, considering undirected attachment score by label (UASL), which assesses the reconstruction accuracy of each syntactic relation separately, establishing important differences among relations that overlap linguistic distinctions. Moreover, we identify two factors that predict most of UASL's variability across relations: (i) the mean and dispersion of the linear distance (on a log scale) between the related words, and (ii) the diversity (similarity-aware entropy) of the syntactic relation's head. These results, which hold across a range of model sizes and architectures, shed light on the degree of abstraction of the representation of syntax in language models and the dependence of such representation on geometric properties of the embedding space.

</details>


### [96] [PersonaEdit: Representative Sample Selection for Personalized Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27816)
*You-Mei Huang,Chung-Chi Chen,An-Zi Yen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaEdit，一种基于隐表示聚类的选择性采样策略，通过比例分层采样挑选代表性编辑样本，使模型编辑技术能够高效扩展到LLM个性化任务，大幅减少所需编辑样本数量，并与检索增强方法互补进一步提升个性化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于检索的个性化方法严重依赖检索质量且在长期交互中性能退化；模型编辑虽在事实知识编辑中表现出色，但直接扩展到个性化面临大量用户数据编辑导致的计算成本高和编辑间干扰问题，亟需有效的样本选择策略。

Method: 提出PersonaEdit框架，采用隐表示聚类策略，通过比例分层采样（proportional stratified sampling）从用户数据中选取代表性编辑样本，从而在减少编辑数量的同时保持个性化性能。此外，探索将模型编辑与检索增强的prompt增强策略相结合。

Result: 实验表明模型编辑对个性化任务是有效的；所提出的选择策略在显著减少编辑样本数量的同时保留大部分性能；模型编辑与检索增强方法结合可进一步提升个性化效果，因为编辑知识与检索上下文提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 模型编辑作为LLM个性化的一种高效且可扩展的方法具有巨大潜力，PersonaEdit的采样策略有效解决了大规模编辑的计算与干扰问题，且编辑与检索的融合可提供更全面的个性化支持。

Abstract: Personalization has attracted growing interest in LLM applications, yet existing retrieval-based approaches depend heavily on retrieval quality and degrade in long-term interactions. Model editing, which directly modifies internal model parameters to incorporate new knowledge, has demonstrated effective knowledge modification capabilities in factual knowledge editing tasks and may provide a potential solution for personalization. However, scaling model editing to personalization is non-trivial. Editing large amounts of user data increases computational cost and causes interference among edits, motivating the need for effective sample selection. To address this issue, we propose, PersonaEdit, a hidden representation clustering strategy that selects representative editing samples through proportional stratified sampling. Experiments show that model editing is effective for personalization, and that our selection strategy preserves most of the performance while substantially reducing the number of required editing samples. Beyond standalone editing, we find that combining model editing with retrieval-based prompt augmentation further improves personalization, as edited knowledge and retrieved context provide complementary information. These results demonstrate the potential of model editing as an efficient and scalable approach for LLM personalization.

</details>


### [97] [Synthetic Linguistic Agency: How an Embodied Mortal Agent Learns Linguistic Affordances through Consequential Social Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27843)
*Sixin Chen,Taizhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从语言能动性（linguistic agency）理论出发，提出合成语言能动性（SLA）的操作性定义，并基于稳态调节强化学习构建了一个具有具身性、社会互动性和脆弱性（即会死亡）的"具身临终代理"（EMA），通过受控实验证明其语言选择受身体状态和社会历史影响、能改变伙伴行为并随经验适应，在操作性定义下表现出SLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型虽然能流利对话并影响人类决策，但其言语交流缺乏一种"持续的、脆弱的、属于自身的生活"。语言学能动性理论将这种缺失的连接识别为语言能动性，其特征包括具身性（拥有承担后果的身体）、语言参与（互动改变双方）和脆弱性（未来可被维系或丧失）。本文旨在探究这种组织能否出现在人工系统中。

Method: 研究包含两个协调部分：(1) 将语言能动性理论的关系翻译为可检查的合成语言能动性（SLA）标准，并识别已有的SLA系统；(2) 基于稳态调节强化学习（Homeostatically Regulated Reinforcement Learning）构建一个以死亡为约束的语言强化学习模型，实例化为具身临终代理（EMA）。EMA学习不同说话方式如何改变伙伴保护它的意愿，并通过权衡这些回应对其剩余生命的意义来选择表达方式。通过受控实验考察其语言选择是否依赖身体状态、社会历史、伙伴行为变化以及经验适应。

Result: 受控实验表明：EMA的语言选择依赖于其身体状态和社会历史，能改变伙伴行为，并能通过与特定伙伴的经验进行适应。当身体后果持续存在时，语言选择改变同一生命体的未来；当身体被重置时，社会效应仍然保留但不再塑造持续存活能力。该EMA在操作性定义下表现出合成语言能动性。

Conclusion: 该工作为合成语言能动性提供了可操作的定义和实证实现，启发未来关于合成共情和策略性人机交互的研究方向：具有持续身体、历史与未来的人工代理如何发展和表达共情，以及人类如何关怀、协商或治理这些代理。

Abstract: Contemporary language models can converse fluently and influence human decisions, yet their exchanges do not enter a continuing, vulnerable life of their own. Linguistic-agency theory identifies this missing connection as linguistic agency and characterizes it through embodiment, linguistic participation, and precariousness: a body that acts and bears consequences, interaction that changes both agent and partner, and a future that can be sustained or lost. Two coordinated studies examine how this organization can appear in artificial systems. First, we translate these relations into inspectable criteria for Synthetic Linguistic Agency (SLA) and identify several existing SLA systems. Second, building on Homeostatically Regulated Reinforcement Learning, we develop a mortality-grounded linguistic-reinforcement-learning model and instantiate it in an Embodied Mortal Agent (EMA). The EMA learns how ways of speaking change a partner's willingness to protect it and chooses expressions by considering what those responses mean for its remaining life. Controlled experiments show that linguistic choices depend on the EMA's body and social history, change partner behavior, and adapt through experience with particular partners. When bodily consequences persist, linguistic choices alter the future of the same life; when the body is reset, their social effects remain but no longer shape continued viability. The resulting EMA exhibits SLA under our operational definition. This work motivates further research on synthetic empathy and strategic human-AI interaction: how artificial agents with persistent bodies, histories, and futures might develop and express empathy, and how people might care for, negotiate with, or govern them.

</details>


### [98] [EvoHarmBench: Breaking Content Moderation with Iterative Human-Like Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27844)
*Ruijie Jian,Benlei Cui,Ting Ma,Haidong Ding,Kangwei Liu,Ziwen Xu,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ziqiang Zhu,Junjie Li,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarmBench，首个面向内容审核系统的动态对抗评估框架，通过迭代优化在语义簇层面演化规避策略，同时优化攻击成功率与人类可读性。实验基于5,002个真实世界对抗样本（覆盖5类违规、229个子簇），发现在12轮优化后，对SOTA LLM审核器的攻击成功率在可读性约束下达80.3%，揭示了现有静态基准与真实部署之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害内容检测评估依赖静态基准，无法反映真实内容平台中用户根据审核反馈持续修改表达的互动对抗生态，导致离线基准分数与在线部署效果之间存在显著性能差距。作者旨在弥合这一差距，推动评估方法论从静态向动态对抗评估转变。

Method: 提出EvoHarmBench动态对抗评估框架，核心是一个迭代优化循环，在语义簇级别演化规避策略（而非单个样本级别），同时联合优化两个目标：规避成功率和人类可读性。该框架被用于系统评估广泛部署于真实审核系统的LLM防御模型。

Result: 评估覆盖5类违规下的229个语义子簇，基于从内容平台收集的5,002个真实对抗样本。实验发现即便是领先的商业系统也存在显著脆弱性：经过12轮优化迭代后，在可读性约束下对SOTA LLM审核器的攻击成功率高达80.3%。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁内容安全研究从静态基准评估转向动态对抗评估范式，并承诺发布完整基准数据、评估框架和代码以推动该转变。核心结论是现有LLM审核系统在动态对抗攻击面前存在严重脆弱性，亟需更贴近真实世界的评估方法。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of harmful content detection rely predominantly on static benchmarks, which struggle to reflect the interactive adversarial ecosystem of real-world content platforms where users continuously revise their expressions in response to moderation feedback. This mismatch creates a significant performance gap between offline benchmark scores and online deployment effectiveness. To the best of our knowledge, we present EvoHarmBench, the first dynamic adversarial evaluation framework for content moderation systems. The framework employs an iterative optimization loop that evolves evasion strategies at the semantic-cluster level, while simultaneously optimizing for evasion success and human readability. We systematically evaluate LLM-based defense models which are widely used in real world moderation systems. The evaluation covers 229 semantic sub-clusters across five violation categories, derived from 5,002 real-world adversarial samples collected from content platforms. Our experiments reveal substantial vulnerabilities even in leading commercial systems: after twelve optimization iterations, the attack success rate under readability constraints reaches 80.3% within SOTA LLM moderators. We will release the full benchmark data, evaluation framework, and code to encourage a shift from static benchmarking toward dynamic adversarial evaluation in content safety research.

</details>


### [99] [AI Writers Have a Consistent Stylometric Footprint, but AI Editors Do Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27855)
*Zhengyang Shan,Yukyung Lee,Sophie Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现AI生成与AI编辑在文体计量学上留下不同痕迹：AI生成文本的特征足迹以熵和词汇多样性为主，可稳定区分于人类写作；而AI编辑文本仅呈现小幅词汇多样性上升和熵下降，其主导信号是词汇密度。因此"AI文本"并非单一现象，生成与编辑需分别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明AI生成文本与人类写作在文体计量学上存在差异，但LLM不仅用于生成文本，也越来越多地用于编辑人类写作。目前不清楚生成与编辑这两种用途是否留下相同的文体痕迹，这可能导致检测方法误判或混淆不同场景下的AI参与程度。

Method: 通过在8个LLM和5个领域中对AI生成文本、AI编辑文本及人类写作进行文体计量学特征分析，比较熵、词汇多样性、词汇密度等特征在不同条件下的分布差异，并评估这些特征在区分三种文本类型（AI生成、AI编辑、人类写作）时的有效性。

Result: AI生成文本具有一致的"文体足迹"，主要由熵和词汇多样性构成，稳定区分生成文本与人类写作；AI编辑文本不复制该足迹，相对人类来源仅小幅增加词汇多样性并降低熵，词汇密度成为编辑相关的主导信号。文体特征能在生成与编辑之间有效区分，但难以在编辑与人类写作之间有效区分。

Conclusion: AI生成与AI编辑留下性质不同的文体计量学痕迹，"AI文本"不是一个单一现象。研究者应将生成与编辑作为两种独立场景分别研究，以便更准确地理解和检测AI在文本创作中的参与。

Abstract: Text generated by large language models (LLMs) has been shown to be stylometrically distinct from human-written text \citep{andreDetectingAIAuthorship2023, shahDetectingUnmaskingAIGenerated2023, oparaStyloAIDistinguishingAIGenerated2024, soto2024fewshot, liLinguisticDifferencesAI2025, selviogluFeatureExtractionAnalysis2025}. But LLMs are increasingly used not only to generate text but also to edit human writing, and it is unclear whether the two leave the same trace. We show that AI generation leaves a consistent ``stylometric footprint'': a small subset of features, primarily entropy and lexical diversity, consistently separates AI-generated text from human writing across 8 LLMs and 5 domains, while the remaining features depend heavily on the domain and generator. AI editing, however, does not reproduce the same footprint. Relative to their human-written sources, AI-edited texts show only a small increase in lexical diversity and a decrease in entropy, rather than the joint increase that characterizes AI generation. Lexical density, which contributes little to generation, instead becomes the dominant editing-associated signal. Stylometric features therefore separate AI-edited text from AI-generated text but are substantially less effective at separating it from human-written text. Our results suggest that ``AI text'' is not a single phenomenon: generation and editing leave qualitatively different stylometric traces and should be studied separately.

</details>


### [100] [OpenStamp: A Watermark for Open-Source Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27899)
*Miroojin Bakshi,Saksham Rastogi,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpenStamp是一种将水印逻辑直接编码到模型权重（仅修改最终投影/unembedding层）的大语言模型水印技术，通过实验证明其在检测性能上优于现有方法，同时最小化对模型能力的损伤，并且对释义攻击和事后微调更具鲁棒性，已发布4个主流开源模型的水印版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token采样概率修改的水印方法不适用于开源模型，因为白盒访问下用户可以轻易在推理阶段禁用水印。需要一种能抵抗用户人为破坏、将水印嵌入模型权重本身的方法。

Method: OpenStamp仅修改模型的最终投影（unembedding）层，将水印逻辑直接编码进模型权重中，而非依赖推理时的采样概率调整。通过这种方式，水印与模型本身绑定，用户即使有白盒访问也无法在推理中轻易禁用。

Result: 在两个模型上的实验表明，OpenStamp实现了优越的水印检测性能，对模型能力的影响极小；且经设计验证，较之前方法对释义攻击和事后微调具有更强的抗清洗鲁棒性；已发布代码及4个流行开源模型的水印版本。

Conclusion: OpenStamp为开源LLM提供了一种更可靠、更抗攻击的水印方案，通过将水印嵌入模型权重来防止用户绕过，是保护开源模型内容归属的有效手段。

Abstract: With the growing prevalence of large language model (LLM) generated content, watermarking is considered a promising approach for attributing text to LLMs and distinguishing it from human-written content. A prominent class of techniques embeds subtle but detectable signals in generated text by modifying token sampling probabilities. However, such methods are unsuitable for open-source models, where users have white-box access and can easily disable watermarking during inference. In this work, we introduce OpenStamp, a watermarking technique that encodes the watermarking logic directly into the model weights by modifying only the final projection, or unembedding, layer. Through experiments across two models, we show that OpenStamp achieves superior detection performance, with minimal degradation in model capabilities compared to prior methods. The implanted watermark is explicitly designed, and empirically confirmed, to be more robust to paraphrasing attacks and harder to scrub off through post-hoc fine-tuning than prior open-source watermarks. To enable developers to watermark their models, we release our code alongside watermarked versions of 4 popular open-source models.

</details>


### [101] [LandingAgent: A Reference-Annotated Dataset and Agentic Generation Framework for Landing Pages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27902)
*Injun Baek,HyeongSeok Lee,Yearim Kim,Junhoo Lee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出LandingBench数据集和LandingAgent框架，用于目标导向、参考引导的着陆页生成，通过三阶段代理流程（目标画像、参考引导线框图、批判式精炼）生成可执行的、高保真的着陆页，避免直接LLM生成带来的通用模板和缺乏说服力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着陆页是具有目标导向的网页界面，需要传达特定价值主张并组织信息流、视觉层次和行动号召（CTA）。虽然LLM能从自然语言提示生成网页代码，但直接生成往往产生通用模板和不支持的说服性声明，缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出了LandingBench数据集（将真实着陆页抽象为章节序列、布局模式、语气描述、视觉强调和CTA结构的参考画像），以及LandingAgent框架——一个三阶段代理系统：①目标画像（profiling the target）、②参考引导线框图构建（reference-guided wireframe）、③批判式引导的页面精炼（critique-guided polishing）。

Result: 与直接提示（direct prompting）相比，LandingAgent在忠实度（faithfulness）、简洁性（conciseness）、可读性（readability）、美学（aesthetics）和结构多样性（structural diversity）方面均有改进，表现出更强的目标针对性、更高的呈现质量和更大的布局多样性。

Conclusion: 参考引导的三阶段代理框架能够有效生成高质量、目标导向的着陆页，解决直接LLM生成的泛化问题，相关工作代码已开源。

Abstract: Landing pages are goal-oriented web interfaces that must communicate a target-specific value proposition while organizing information flow, visual hierarchy, and calls to action (CTA). Although large language models can generate plausible webpage code from natural-language prompts, direct generation often yields generic templates and unsupported persuasive claims. We study target-grounded, reference-guided landing-page generation, where a system must create an executable page for a new target by adapting reusable patterns from real pages without copying them. We introduce LandingBench, a reference-profile dataset that abstracts real landing pages into section sequences, layout patterns, tone descriptors, visual emphasis, and CTA structure. Building on LandingBench, we propose LandingAgent, a three-phase agentic framework that profiles the target, constructs a reference-guided wireframe, and refines the page through critique-guided polishing. We evaluate LandingAgent against direct prompting on faithfulness, conciseness, readability, aesthetics, and structural diversity. Experiments show improved target grounding, presentation quality, and layout diversity. Code is available at https://github.com/IAURAI/LandingAgent.

</details>


### [102] [What Makes Agent Memory Useful for Reliable Unanswerable Question Handling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27924)
*Chuanyuan Tan,Junjie Yu,Yuxin Wang,Yining Zheng,Xipeng Qiu,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了智能体记忆在不可回答问题（UAQ）处理中的可靠性作用。在统一的智能体RAG框架下，评估了四种代表性记忆方法在三个UAQ数据集和两个基础模型上的表现，发现记忆对UAQ性能的提升具有选择性而非普遍性，跨模型记忆复用比跨数据集迁移更可行，并且程序性和规则型记忆最可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可回答问题（UAQ）的可靠处理对基于LLM的可信智能体至关重要。虽然记忆在智能体系统中被广泛使用，但记忆在UAQ可靠处理中的具体作用此前尚不明确，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在统一的智能体RAG框架下，系统比较了四种代表性记忆方法，使用三个UAQ相关数据集和两个基础模型进行评估。分析了记忆在不同设置下的效果、跨模型与跨数据集的迁移能力，以及不同记忆表示形式（程序性、规则型、轨迹型等）对UAQ处理的影响。

Result: 记忆在部分设置下能改善UAQ性能，但增益具有选择性且在数据集迁移下表现脆弱。跨模型记忆复用比跨数据集迁移更可行，表明可回答性模式的转变比基础模型变化更影响记忆复用。UAQ增益更强烈地通过决策引导而非轨迹塑形得以保留。程序性和规则型记忆提供最可靠的支持，组合记忆中最有效的方式是将程序性引导与互补行为信号结合。

Conclusion: 可靠的UAQ记忆不依赖于存储更多经验，而在于保留可迁移的行为引导。研究为智能体系统中记忆设计和UAQ处理提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Reliable handling of unanswerable questions (UAQs) is critical for trustworthy LLM-based agents. Although memory is widely used in agent systems, its role in reliable UAQ handling remains unclear. We present a systematic study of agent memory for UAQ handling under a unified agentic RAG framework, evaluating four representative memory methods across three UAQ-related datasets and two base models.
  We find that memory can improve UAQ performance in some settings, but such gains are selective rather than universal and remain fragile under dataset shift. Interestingly, cross-model memory reuse is often more feasible than cross-dataset transfer, suggesting that shifts in answerability patterns pose a greater challenge to memory reuse than changes in the base model itself. We further find that UAQ gains are more strongly preserved through decision guidance than through trajectory shaping, and that memory effectiveness depends strongly on representation. In particular, procedural and rule-based memories often provide the most reliable support for UAQ handling, while memory composition is most effective when procedural guidance is combined with complementary behavioral signals. Overall, our findings suggest that reliable UAQ memory depends less on storing larger amounts of experience and more on preserving transferable behavioral guidance.

</details>


### [103] [Entity-Memory Graph Retrieval Improves Evidence Coverage in Long-Conversation Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27925)
*Shumao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究实体-记忆图检索（Entity-Memory graph retrieval）在多轮对话问答中的效果。该图结构以对话轮次为记忆节点、共享实体连接重复提及、有向时间边连接相邻记忆。在10个LoCoMo对话的1,986个问题上，图检索将top-k 25官方证据召回率从79.75%提升至84.48%，且在top-k 5到50范围内召回优势一致，但最终答案F1无显著差异。作者通过严格匹配的对照实验隔离了图结构本身带来的检索覆盖增益，但未建立最终答案F1增益、模型/嵌入等价性或跨数据集泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集向量余弦排序的检索器在多轮对话中可能遗漏相邻记忆中重要信息，因为对话具有时间顺序和实体重复连接的结构特性。作者旨在探究图结构是否能在不改变读取器和查询/记忆向量的前提下，带来检索覆盖率的提升。

Method: 构建实体-记忆图：对话轮次作为verbatim记忆节点，共享实体连接重复提及，相邻记忆用有向时间边连接。查询时按四步流水线检索：实体门控（entity gating）→ 语义融合（semantic fusion）→ 一跳时间恢复（one-hop chronological recovery）→ 密集回填（dense backfill）。采用匹配的密集对照组，共享相同的记忆/查询向量、上下文预算、回答协议和评估器，从而将图结构的影响与读取器变化隔离。

Result: 在10个LoCoMo对话的1,986个问题上，官方证据top-k 25召回率从79.7468%提升至84.4842%；top-k 5至50均支持召回优势。但所有匹配阈值下最终答案F1均无显著差异。四种论文符合条件的请求配置在GPT-3.5和DeepSeek提取器上均验证了鲁棒性。嵌入鲁棒性呈混合结果：F1无显著对比，但召回对嵌入伪影敏感。

Conclusion: 该实验隔离了图结构带来的检索覆盖增益，证明图结构能保留密集余弦排序可能遗漏的相邻记忆。但研究不足以证明最终答案F1增益、模型或嵌入的等价性，也不能推广到跨数据集场景。结论应限定为：图结构在检索覆盖率上有增益，但对下游最终答案质量的影响未被确立。

Abstract: Entity-Memory graph retrieval keeps dialogue turns as verbatim Memory nodes, links repeated mentions through shared Entities, and connects adjacent Memories with directed chronological edges. At query time the retriever moves from Entity gating through semantic fusion and one-hop chronological recovery to dense backfill. The path can keep a neighboring Memory that dense cosine ranking would otherwise omit. A matched dense control shares the Memory and query vectors, context budget, requested answer protocol, and evaluator, isolating graph structure from changes to the reader.
  On 1,986 questions from ten LoCoMo conversations, graph retrieval raises official evidence recall at top-k 25 from 79.7468% to 84.4842%. The recall advantage is supported from top-k 5 to 50, while no matched cutoff supports an overall final-answer F1 difference. Four paper-eligible requested configurations support empirical robustness across the tested GPT-3.5 and DeepSeek extractors on both outcomes. Embedding robustness is mixed: F1 has no supported contrast, but recall is sensitive to the embedding artifact. The comparison isolates a retrieval-coverage gain from graph structure. It does not establish a final-answer F1 gain, model or embedding equivalence, or cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [104] [Lexically conditioned realization ambiguity in Korean predicate morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27966)
*Wonjun Oh,KyungTae Lim,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL
